# Aspects of the mixed signalling on routes built by hand, written by test_signalling_aspects.
# A line is: <group> <parameters> | <one token per section in route order>. A token is code:state:signal:braking:exit.
#   code     Section::code
#   state    G green, Y yellow, R red, RR red_red; any other state text as it is
#   signal   signalSpeedLimit of the arcs of the section in arc order, joined by '/'; one value when all arcs have it
#   braking  speedInBraking of the arcs of the section in arc order, joined by '/'
#   exit     exit_speed of the section
# Parameters: levels has one character per section (u = no level, 0 to 5 = the level) and a second route follows after '/';
#   shape is the number of arcs of the sections (1 = one each, 21 = 2 1 2 1 ..., 12 = 1 2 1 2 ...);
#   occ lists the occupied sections by index, connected lists the ids in BlocksConnected, left is the id given to
#   relLastSectionMixedSignalling, held is singleTrackHeld, signals are signalCode1/signalCode2.
# The ids of the sections are s0, s1 ... in route order. A second route lists the ids in reverse order; ' || ' separates the routes.
# Start states. Clean: code 270, state green, signal speed limit 999 on every arc, braking 0, exit 0.
#   Dirty: code 11, state ?, signal speed limit 7 on every arc, braking 0, exit 5.
#   No routine writes the dirty values, so a section that no routine wrote still shows them.
# Speed limit of arc a of section i in m/s: {36, 33, 30, 8, 27, 24, 21}[i] - 2 * a.
#   They are numbers of the test, not railway data.
# signalCode1 = 11.111 and signalCode2 = 0 in every group except V.
# Groups:
#   U  one level on all sections, one step
#   B  two levels with the border at section b, one occupied section h with b <= h
#   T  two levels with the border at section 3, two occupied sections
#   R  three or more levels on one route
#   S  a train of two sections moves through a route, one step per line, clean start
#   L  only the release
#   E  the release of the last section of the route
#   V  other signal speeds
#   K  a held single-track zone seen from two routes
# U: one level on all sections, one step, dirty start
